Title: Kanonikai diataxeis, epistolai, lyseis, thespismata ton hagiotaton patriarchon Konstantinoupoleos apo Gregoriou tou Theologou mechri Dionysiou tou apo Adrianoupoleos (canonical ordinances, letters, rulings and decrees of the most holy patriarchs of Constantinople, from Gregory the Theologian to Dionysius of Adrianople); Volume One
Creator: the patriarchs of Constantinople; collected under the direction of Manouel Io. Gedeon
Publisher: En Konstantinoupolei: ek tou Patriarchikou Typographeiou
Publication Date: 1888
Rights: Public Domain (published 1888, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: grc
Source: Internet Archive item kanonikaidiataxe01gede (https://archive.org/details/kanonikaidiataxe01gede), the item's own kanonikaidiataxe01gede_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Robarts - University of Toronto; host metadata date: 1888. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 38bf27fc771f7fe00ebee2e742e7b8df1035210191129bb7ae14fa21be8fc2ab.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. University of Toronto (Robarts) scan of the Patriarchal Press edition. The OCR drops accents and breathings and turns some letters into Latin look-alikes, but the running text reads.
Content note: Gedeon's collection, printed at the Patriarchate's own press, of canonical ordinances, letters and synodal rulings of the patriarchs of Constantinople, most of them from manuscripts and many from the Ottoman centuries: dowry and marriage rulings, penances lifted, monastic and parish discipline, with acts of the eighteenth century down to Gregory V's first patriarchate (1798) and later patriarchs. Each document ends with a note on where it was found.
